## Constants

Bytes and words the code reads directly (see calibration.md). * marks a structural name. Value is
the raw value; Physical applies the unit. For option bytes the bits tested are listed, with the
state in this image (1 = set).

| Address | Name | Category | Size | Value | Physical | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| $0305 | ChecksumTarget | Diagnostics | 1 | $17 (23) |  | Checksum check (ChecksumCheck, P0601): the 8-bit sum of the whole image must equal this byte, the last byte of the part number in the header; the correction byte is $030F (tools/fixsum.py) |
| $0315 | ObdStandardIndex | Diagnostics | 1 | $02 (2) |  | Index into the OBD-requirements value list at $32178 sent for mode $01 PID $1C (7 selects $01, OBD II as defined by CARB) |
| $0316 | CalTypeCode | Startup | 1 | $08 (8) |  | Header byte (7): copied to $0059,Z at reset, plus 4 when it is 5 or more and Options032E bit 7 is set (no reader of $0059 found) |
| $0320 | BusMsgAcData | Other | 2 | $0DB0 (3504) |  | Data word sent in the bus message $AC (CcdTransmit; bit 3 set with Options032E bit 7) |
| $0322 | BusMsgEcData | Other | 1 | $64 (100) |  | Data byte sent in the bus message $EC (Sub_30972; low two bits replaced) |
| $0323 | BusInfoByte1 | Other | 1 | $21 (33) |  | First data byte of the bus information message built by Sub_31860 (after $06 $ED) |
| $0324 | BusInfoByte2 | Other | 1 | $18 (24) |  | Second data byte of the bus information message |
| $0325 | BusInfoByte3 | Other | 1 | $44 (68) |  | Third data byte of the bus information message (also read by the per-event code) |
| $0326 | BusInfoByte4 | Other | 1 | $18 (24) |  | Fourth data byte of the bus information message |
| $0327 | BusInfoByte5 | Other | 1 | $0B (11) |  | Fifth data byte of the bus information message (bit 6 added with calibration bit $032E.7) |
| $0328 | Options0328 | Options | 1 | $00 (0) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01=0, $02=0. |
| $032A | Options032A | Options | 1 | $40 (64) |  | Option byte; bit tests in the reference-event and spark code (bits 7-6: spark cap and ignition variants) |
| $032B | Options032B | Options | 1 | $00 (0) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $20 SparkCalcHookOn=0, $40 DwellBatteryOn=0, $80=0. |
| $032C | Options032C | Options | 1 | $00 (0) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $40 FlexFuelOn=0, $80=0. |
| $032D | Options032D | Options | 1 | $04 (4) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $02=0, $04=1, $08=0, $10=0, $18=0, $20 AcClutchLogicOn=0, $40=0, $80=0. |
| $032E | Options032E | Options | 1 | $3E (62) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $04=1, $08=1, $10=1, $20=1, $40 ManualTransmission=0, $80=0. |
| $032F | Options032F | Options | 1 | $84 (132) |  | Option byte; bit tests in the event, fuel and closed-loop code (bits 2-1: output timing set-up) |
| $0330 | Options0330 | Options | 1 | $22 (34) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01 BattTempModelOn=0, $02=1, $04 BattTempCurveAlt=0, $08=0, $10=0, $20 TipInAirTempCheck=1. |
| $0331 | Options0331 | Options | 1 | $D8 (216) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $08=1, $10=1, $20=0, $40=1, $80 AcPressureSensor=1. |
| $0332 | Options0332 | Options | 1 | $08 (8) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01=0, $02=0, $04=0, $08=1, $10=0, $18=1. |
| $0333 | Options0333 | Options | 1 | $84 (132) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01=0, $02=0, $04=1, $10=0, $20=0, $40=0, $80=1. |
| $0334 | Options0334 | Options | 1 | $22 (34) |  | Option byte (bit 4: output-compare set-up at reset and in the PIT code) |

| $0337 | Options0337 | Options | 1 | $E4 (228) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01 IdleDipKickMode=0, $02=0, $04=1, $08=0, $10 SurgeSparkOn=0, $20 SpeedLimitOn=1, $40 SpeedDampOn=1, $80 EventSparkOn=1. |
